No. OSR/5.—In exercise of the powers conferred by 
Section 19 of the Bunking Companies (Acquisition & Trans¬ 
fer of Undertakings) Act, 1970 (5 of 1970), the Board of 
Directors of Union Bank of India in consultation with the 
Reserve Bank of India and with the previous sanction of the 
Central Government hereby makes the following Regulations, 
further to amend Union Bank of India (Officers’) Service 
Regulations, 1979. 


(a) The increments specified in the 

scabs of pay set out in Regul¬ 
ation 4 (1) shall, subject 

to tho sanction of the 
Competent Althority, accrue on 
an annual basis and shall be 
granted on the first day of the 
month in which theso fall due. 

(b) Officers in Scales and Scab 
II, 1 year after reaching the 
maximum in their respective 
scales, shall be granted further 
incremets including stagnation 
increment (s) in tho next higher 
scale only as specified in (c) 
below subject to their crossing 
the efficiency bar- 


2. Short title and commencement.—(1) These Regulations 
may be called Union Bank of India (Officers) Service Re¬ 
gulations, 1979, (2) They shall come into force on the data 
of their publication in the Official Gazette.

No. 28-RC(2)/25/90.—In pursuance of Regulation 159- 
(1) of the' Chartered Accountants Regulations, 1988, the 
Council of the Institute of Chartered Accountants of India 
is pleased to notify the setting up of a brunch of the 
Southern India Regional Council at Tuticorin with effect 
from 1st October, 1990. 

The Branch shall bo known as Tuticorin Branch of the 
Southern India Regional Council. 

As prescribed under Regulation 159(3), the branch shall 
function subject to the control, supervision and direction of 
the Council through the Regional Council and shall carry 
out such directions as may from time to time be issued by 
the Council. 

No. 28-RC (3) 24/90,—In pursuance of Regulation 159 
(1) of the Chartered Regulations, 1988, the Council of the 
Institute of Chartered Accountants of India is pleased to 
notify the setting up of a branch of the Eastern India 
Regional Council at Siliguri with effect from 1st October, 
1990, 

The Branch shall be known as Siliguri Branch of the 
Eastern India Regional Council. 

As prescribed under Regulation 159 (3), the branch shall 
function subject to the control, supervision and direction of 
the Council through the Regional Council and shall carry 
out such directions as may from time to time be issued by 
the Council. 

No. 28-RC (4)/23/90.—In pursuance of Regulation 159 
(1) of the Chartered Accountants Regulations, 1988, the 
Council of the Institute of Chartered Accounants of India 
is ploascd to notify the setting up of a branch of the Contral 
India Regional Council at Ajmer with effect from 1st October 
1990. 

The Branch shall be known as Ajmer Branch of the Central 
India Regional Council. 

AS prescribed under Regulation 159(3), the branch shall 
function subject to the control, supervision and direction of 
the Council trough the Regional Council and shall carry out 
such directions as may from time to time be issued by the 
Council.

MINISTRY OF LABOUR 

OFFICE OF THE CENTRAL PROVIDENT FUND 
COMMISSIONER 

New Delhi-110 001, the 8th October 1990 

No. 2/1959/E.D.L.I/Exemp /89/Pt/5462.—WHEREAS 

M/s. Cosmopolitan Hospitals (P) Ltd., Murinjapalam Pattom, 
P.O. Trivandrum-695004 Code No. KR/10338 have applied 
for exemption under sub-Section 2(A) of Section 17 of the 
Employees' Provident Funds and Miscellaneous Provisions 
Act, 1952 (19 of 1952) (hereinafter referred to as the said 
Act) : 

AND WHEREAS, I, B. N. Som, Central Provident Fund 
Commissioner is satisfied that the employees of the said esta¬ 
blishment is, without making any separate contribution or 
payment of premium, in enjoyment of benefits under the 
Group Insurance Scheme of the Life Insurance Corporation 
of India'in the nature of Life Insurance which are more 
favourable to such employees than the benefits admissible 
under the Employees’ Deposit Linked Insurance Scheme, 1976 
(hereinafter referred to as the said Scheme) : 

NOW, THEREFORE, in exercise of the power conferred 
by sub-Section (2A) of Section 17 of the said Act and subject 
to the conditions specified in Schedule II annexed hereto, I, 
B. N. Som, hereby exempt the above said establishment with 
retrospective effect from which date relaxation order under 
para 28(7) of the said Scheme has been granted by the 
Regional Provident Fund Commissioner Kerala from the 
operation of the said Scheme for and upto a period of 
1-9-87 to 28-2-90. 


SCHEDULE 

1. The employer in relation of the said establishment 
(hereinafter referred to as the employer) shall submit 
such returns to the Regional Provident Fund Commissioner 
concerned and maintain such accounts and provide such faci¬ 
lities for inspection, as the Central Provident Fund Commis¬ 
sioner may direct from time to time. 

2. The employer shall pay such inspection charges as the 
Central Government may, from time to time, direct under 
clause (a) of sub-section (3A) of Section 17 of the said Act, 
within 15 days from the close of every month. 

3. All expenses involved in the administration of the Group 
Insurance Scheme, including maintenance of accounts, sub¬ 
mission of returns, payment of insurance premia, transfer of 
accounts, payment of inspection charges etc. shall be borne 
by the employer. 

4. The employer shall display on the Notice Board of the 
establishment, a copy of the rules of the Group Insurance 
Scheme as approved by the Central Govemment/Central 
Provident Fund Commissioner as and when amended, along- 
with translation of the salient features thereof in the language 
of the majority of the employees. 

5. Whereas an employee, who is already a member of the 
Employees' Provident Fund or the Provident Fund of an 
establishment exempted under the said Act, is employed in 
his establishment the employer shall immediately admit him 



2991 


Part HI-Sec. 4] THE GAZETTE OF INDIA, OCTOBER 27, 1990 (KARTIKA 5, 1912) 


as a member of the Group Insurance Scheme and pay neces¬ 
sary premium in respect of him to the Life Insurance Cor¬ 
poration of India. 

6. The employer shall' arrange to enhance the benefits 
available to the employees under the Group Insurance Scheme 
appropriately if the benefits available to the employees under 
the said Scheme are enhanced so that the benefits available 
under the Group Insurance Scheme are more favourable to 
the employees than the benefits admissible under the said 
Scheme. 

7. Notwithstanding anything contained in the Group Insu¬ 
rance Scheme, if on the death of an employees the amounts 
payable under the Scheme be less than the amount that would 
be payable had the employees been covered under the said 
Scheme, the employer shall pay the difference to the 
nominee(s) /legal heir(s) of the employee as compensation. 

8. No amendment of the provisions of the Group Insurance 
Scheme shall be made without the prior approval of the 
Regional Provident Fund Commissioner concerned and where 
any amendment is likely to effect adversely the interest of 
the employees, the Regional Provident Fund Commissioner 
shall before giving his approval, give a reasonable opportu¬ 
nity to the employee to explain their point of view. 

9. Where for any reason, the employees of the said esta¬ 
blishment do not remain covered under the Group Insurance 
Scheme of the Life Insurance Corporation of India as already 
adopted by the said establishment, or the benefits to the 
employees under this Scheme are reduced in any manner, the 
exemption shall be liable to be cancelled. 

10. Where for any reason, the employer fails to pay the 
premium etc. within the due date, as fixed by the Life Insu¬ 
rance Corporation of India, and the policy is allowed to 
lapse, the exemption shall be liable to be cancelled, 

11. In case of default, if any made by the employer 
payment of promium the responsibility for payment 


assurance benefits to the nominee!®) /legal heir(s) of 
deceased member who would have been covered under the 
said Scheme but for grant of this exemption, shall be that of 
the employer, 

12. Upon the death of the member covered under the 
Group Insurance Scheme the Life Insurance Corporation of 
India shall ensure prompt payment of the sum assured to the 
nominee(s)/Legal heirfs) of the deceased member entitled 
for it and in any case within one month from the receipt of 
claims complete in all respect.

THE BAR COUNCIL OF INDIA 
New Delhi, the 7th December 1989 

Rules in Part IX of the Rules of the Bar Council of India 
containing the fees leviable under the Advocate Act, have been 
amended by tho Bar Council of India (by Resolution No. 
31/89) revising fees payable as given below, The revision of 
fees will come into force from 1st June, 1989. 

1 (a) Petition challenging the election of one or all mem¬ 
bers of the Stale Council—Rs. 500,00, 

lb) Complaint of professional misconduct under section 
35 of the Act—Rs. 100.00. 

Provided that no fee shall be payable on a complaint made 
by any court or tribunal or other statutory body or where in 
a proper case the Bar Council grants exemption therefor. 

(b-1) The complainant shall be liable to pay in addition 
to the fee in sub-clause (b) the cost of service of process 
unless the Disciplinary Committee grants exemption therefor. 

(c) Certificate as to the date of enrolment and the con¬ 
tinuance of the name of the Advocate on the roll—Rs. 25.00, 

(d) Certificate required to be produced with the transfer 
Application under section 18 of the Act—Rs. 50.00. 


(e) Inspection by complainant or the concerned advocates 
of documents relating to tho disciplinary matters—Rs. 10.00. 

(f) Inspection of the roll of the Advocates or the voters 
list—Rs. 10.00. 

(g) Any application made in any proceedings before a 
State Council or its Committee other than the Disciplinary 
Committee-—Rs. 10.00. 

A fee as specified hereunder shall be paid in the following 
matters. 


2 (a) Complaint of professional misconduct preferred under 
section 36 of the Act—Rs. 100,00. 

Provided that no fee shall be payable on a complaint or 
appeal made by any court or tribunal or other statutory body 
or where in a proper case the Council grants exemption 
therefor. 

(a-1) The complainant shall be liable to pay in addition to 
(he fee in sub-clause (a) the cost of service of process unless 
the Disciplinary Committee grants exemption thereof. 

(b) An appeal filed under section 37 of the Act—Rs. 100.00. 

(c) An application for stay made to the Council under the 
Act—Rs. 50,00. 

(d) (i) An application for inspection—Rs. 10.00. 

(.it) Inspection in cases finally decided when per¬ 
mitted—Rs. 25.00. 

(e) An application for withdrawal under Section 36 of the 
Act—Rs. 50.00. 

(I) An application for Review under the Act—Rs. 100.00. 

(g) An application for the exercise of its power under 
Section 48A of the Act (Revision)—Rs, 100.00. 

(h) Any application made in any proceedings before the 
Council or a Committee other than Disciplinary Com¬ 
mittee.—Rs. 10.00. 

3 (a) Every application for an authenticated copy at any 
certificate, order of other proceedings, entry on any roll, or 
any document or deposition in any proceeding, before a 
State Council or the Council or a Committee thereof shall 
be accompanied by a fee of Rs. 10/- and the copying charges 
as follows :— 

Every exemplification of the order or the document in 
addition to the folio and other charges—Rs. 20/-. 

Copying charges for folio—Rs. 10/-. 

A folio shall be deemed to consist of two hundred words, 
seven figures shall be counted as one word, and more than 
half a folio shall be reckoned as a folio. 

(b) In any proceedings, summons to witnesses shall only 
be issued on payment of the requisite batta and/or charge 
accordnig to the rates prescribed by the High Court, in the 
case of a State Council, and the Supreme Court in the Case 
of the Council, or a Committee thereof as the case may be. 

(c) Every interlocutory application, includnig a petition for 
excusing delay or for obtaining stay of proceedings of a Dis¬ 
ciplinary Committee shall be accompanied by a fee of 
Rs. 25/- in the caso of the Disciplinary Committee of a State 
Council and a fee of Rs. 25/- in the case of the Disciplinary 
Committee of the Council, 

(d) An application by a party to the proceedings for un¬ 
attested copies of depositions of witnesses may be supplied at 
the rate or Rs. 5/- for each page of the deposition. 

4. Application for transfer from one State Bar Council to 
another State Bar Council—Rs. 10.00. 

Sd/- ILLEGIBLE 
Secretary 
Bar Council of India

UNIT TRUST OF INDIA 
Bombay, the 7th September 1990 

No. UT/147/DPD/(P&R)117/Vol. 1/90-91.—The provisions 
ctf the 5 year and 7 year Deferred Income Unit Scheme for¬ 
mulated under Section 21 of the Unit Trust of India Act, 
1963 and approved bv the Executive Committee in the meet¬ 
ing held on August 6, 1990 are published herebelow for in¬ 
formation. 

7—YEAR DEFERRED INCOME UNIT SCHEME 

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 21 of the 
Unit Trust of India Act, 1963 (52 of 1963), the Board of the 
Unit Trust of India hereby makes the following Unit Scheme : 

I. Short Title and commencement : 

(1) The Scheme shall be called “7-Year Deferred Income 
Unit Scheme'’. 

(2) It shall come into force from August 16, 1990 shall be 
for a period of 7 years. 

(3) The offer of units under the scheme will be open for 
23 months from August 16. 1990 to October 31, 1990 (both 
days inclusive). Provided that the Chairman or the Executive 
Trustee mav suspend the offer of units under the scheme at 
any time before the expiry of the aforesaid period or extend 
the period of offer beyond the aforesaid period by giving a 
week’s notice in such newspapers as may be decided. 

II. Definitions • — 

In this Scheme, unless the context otherwise requires— 

(a) The "Act’’ means the Unit Trust of Thdia Act, 
1963; 

(b) "acceptance date” with reference to an application 
made by an applicant to the Trust for sale of units 
by the Trust means the day on which the Trust, after 
being satisfied that such application is in order 
accepts the same; 

(c) “Applicant’’ under the scheme shall mean and in¬ 
clude individuals and eligible Instituions as more 
particularly described in paragraph TV hereof. 

(d) “eligible Institutions” means an eligible Trust defined 
under the Unit Trust of India General Regulations 
1964 and shall include private trust created by an 
instrument in writing and Which is irrevocable, 

(e) “number of units" to be issued means the aggregate 
of the number of units sold and outstanding. 


(f) ‘ “recognised Stock exchange" means a Stock ex¬ 
change which is, for the time being recognised under 
the Securities Contracts (Regulation) Act, 1956 (42 
of 1956); 

(g) ‘Regulations” means Unit Trust of India General 
Regulations, 1964 made under Section 43 (1) of the 
Act; 

(h) “Society" means a Society Registered under the 
Societies Registration Act of 1860 or any other 
society established under any state or central law for 
the time being in force. 

(i) “Unit" means one undivided share of the face value 
of Rupees ten in the unit capital. 

(i) all other expressions not defined herein but defined 
in the Act shall have the respective meanings assign¬ 
ed to them by the Act. 

III. Face Value of each Unit :— 

The face value of each unit shall bo ten rupees. 

IV. Application for units : 

(1) Application for units may be made by residents only 
viz. 

(a) individuals either singly or with another individual 
on joint/either or survivor basis. 

lb) a parent, step-parent or other lawful guardian on 
behalf of a minor. An application cannot be made 
by an adult and minor jointly. 

(c) an eligible institution as defined under the scheme; 

(d) a society as defined under the scheme. 

(2) Applicatioos shall bo made in such form as may be 
approved by the Chairman/Executive Trustee Of the Trust. 

(3) The payment for thp units applied for by an eligible 
person shall be made by him along with the, application in 
cash, cheque or draft. Cheques and drafts should be drawn 
on branches of banks within the city where the office at which 
the application is tendered is situated. If the payment is 
made by cheque, the acceptance date will subject to such 
cheque being realised, be the date on which the cheque is 
received by the Trust or by a branch of a designated bank. 
Tf pavment is made bv draft the acceptance date will, sublect 
to such draft being realised, be the date of Issue of such draft 
provided the application is received by the Trust or a branch 
of a designated bank within a reasonable time. 

(4) A unit certificate will be sent bv registered post with 
or without acknowledgement due to the address given bv tb>* 
annlieant and the Trust will not incur anv liability for the 
loss, damage, misdelivery, or nOn-delivery of the certificate, 
so sent. 

V. Offer of units 

The offer of units under the Scheme shall bo open for a 
period of 23 months commencing from August 16, 199(3 to 
October 31, 1990 (both days inclusive). The Trust reserves 
the right to close the offer before the stipulated date. Appli¬ 
cations received after the close of business hours on 31st Oc¬ 
tober, 1990 and subsequently at any of the offices of the Trust 
shall be deemed invalid and rejected. 

VT. Repurchase of units ;— 

(1) The Trust shall not repurchase units during the currency 
of the scheme, 

VII. Restrictions on offer and repurchase of units :— 

Notwithstanding anything contained in any provision of 
the Scheme, the Trust shall not be under an obligation to 
offer or finally repurchase units— 

(i) on such days as are not working days; and 

(ii) during the period when the register of Unitholders is 
closed in connection with (as notified by the Trust) 
the annual closing of the books and accounts.

(1) The units shall be offered at Rs. 10 /- during August 16, 
1990 to 31st October, 1990. 

(2) In the event of a termination of the scheme in the 
manner as specified in Clause XXV hereof the Trust shall 
determine the repurchase price by valuing the assets pertaining 
to the schemes as at the close of business on the date notified 
for termination reduced by the liabilities pertaining to the 
scheme and dividing them by the number of units outstanding 
and deducting therefrom such sum as in the opinion of the 
Trust is adequate to cover brokerage, commission taxes, if any. 
stamp duties and other charges in relation to realisation of 
investments bv the Trust and other adlustments and the distri¬ 
bution to the unitholders of the assets in respect of the 
scheme. In such an event the repurchase price shall bear in 
addition to the par value the other distributable component of 
the asset per unit arrived bv the Trust in a manner satisfac¬ 
tory to its auditors and as the Board may approve.

fa) Uoon termination of the Scheme in the manner pm-

X. Valuation of assets pertaining lo this Scheme : — 

(1) For the purpose of valuation of assets under sub-clam e 
(2) of Clause IX. the assets shall be classified into (a) cash, 
lb) investments, and fc) other assets. 

(2) Investments shall he valued by taking : 

A, (a) the closing prices on the slock exchange as on the 
working day on which the valuation is made of the securities 
held by the Trust pertaining to this Scheme, provided where a 
security is quoted on more than one stock exchange the man¬ 
ner of determining the price of such security shall be decided 
bv the Trust. 

fb) where nnv investment was not. during the relevant 
period, dealt in. or quoted on anv recognised stock exchange, 
such value, as the Trust mav, in the circumstances consider to 
be fair value of such investment, and 

B, fa) In the case of interest earning deposits, interest 
accrued but dot received. 

(b) In the ease of Government securities and debentures, 
interest accrued hut not received, and 

fc) In the case of preference shaves and couitv shares 
quoted ex-dividend, any dividend declared but not received. 

(31 Other .v-sels shall be valued at their book value. 

XI. Form of unit certificate : 

Unit Certificates shall be in Form A annexed hereto. Each 
unit certificate shall hear a distinctive number, the number of 
units represented bv the certificate and the name of the unit- 
holder. 

XU. Manner of preparatitm of unit certificate ;— 

The Unit Certificates may be engraved or lithographed or

XIII, Trust not to he recognised regarding unit certificates 

The person who is registered as the holder and in whose 
name a unit certificate has been issued shall be the only person 
to be recognised bv the Trust as the unitholder and as having 
a right, title or interest in or to such unit certificate and the 
units which it represents; and the Trust mav recognise such 
unitholder as the absolute owner thereof and shall not be 
bound bv anv notice to the contrary or to take notice of the 
execution of anv trust or save as herin expressly provided or 
as bv some court of competent jurisdiction affecting the title 
to anv unit certificate or the units thereby represented. 

XIV, Exchange of Unit Certificate and procedure when the 

certificate is mutilated, defaced, lost etc. 

(1) fn case anv unit certificate shall be mutilated or worn 
or defaced, the Trust at its discretion may issue to the person 
entitled a new unit certificate representing the same aggregate 
number of units as the mutilated or worn or defaced unit 
certificate. In case any unit certificate should be lost, stolen 
or destroyed, the Trust mav, at its discretion, issue to the 
person entitled a new certificate in lieu thereof no such new 
certificate shall be issued unless the applicant shall previously 
have. 

fi) Furnished to the Trust evidence satisfactory to it of 
the mutilation, wearing out, defacement loss, theft 
or destruction of the original unit certificate. 

(ii) Paid all expenses in connection with the investigation 
of the facts. 

(iii) In case of mutilation or wearing out or defacement 
produced and surrendered to the Trust the mutilated 
or worn out or defaced unit certificate; and 

i iv) furnished to the Trust such indemnity as it may re¬ 
quire. The Trust shall not incur anv liability for 
issuing such certificate in good faith under the pro¬ 
visions of this clause. 

(2) Before issuing any certificate under the provisions of 
this clause, the Trust may require the applicant for the unit 
certificate to nav a fee of Runee one per unit certificate, issued 
by it together with a sum sufficient in the opinion of the Trust 
to cover stamp duly, if any, or other charges or taxes includ¬ 
ing postal registration charges that may be payable in connec¬ 
tion with the issue and dispatch Of such certificate. 

XV, Register of unitholders — 

The following provisions shall have effect with regard fo 
the registration of unitholders : 

fl) A register of unitholders shall be kept by the Trust at 
its Head Office and there shall be entered in the register : 

fn) the name and addresses of the unitholders; 

(b) the distinctive number of the unit certificate and the 
number of units held by every such person; and 

fc) the date on which such person became the holder of 
the units in his nnme. 

(2) (a) If a unit certificate stands registered in the name 
of two persons, such persons shall he deemed to hold the 
certificates jointly and a discharge bv the person first named 
in the register of the unitholders shall, ns regards receipt of 
amounts due in respect of such units, discharge the Trust in 
respect of such amounts. 

i h) Where two individuals, none of them being a minor, 
apply for issue of a unit continuously in their favour and 
reouest in the application that either of them should be per¬ 
mitted to deal with the units, the Trust shall record iii its 
honks suitable entries to take note of such requests and whrn 
a unit certificate has been issued in such circumstances, then

XVI. Receipt by unitholder to discharge Trust : 

The receipt of the unitholder for any moneys paid to him 
in respect of the units represented hv the certificate shall be a 
good discharge to the Trust. 

XVIT. Death of bankruptcy of a unitholder : 

(11 In case of death of either of the Joint holders of a unit 
certificate, the survivor shall be the only person recognised hv 
the Trust as haying title to or interest in the units represented 
bv 1 the unit certificate. Provided that nothing herein contained 
shall effect anv right which anv other person may have ns 
against such survivor in respect of the said units. 

(2) In the event of death of a single holder, the nominee 
shall be the person recognised by the Trust as the person en¬ 
titled to the amount payable by the Trust in respect of units 
under the Regulations. 

'3) In the absence of a valid nomination by a single unit- 
holder, the executor or administrators of the deceased unit- 
holder, or a holder of succession certificate issued under Part 
X of the Indian Succession Act. 1925 (39 of 19251 shall he 
the only persons who may be recognised bv theTrust as hav¬ 
ing any title to the unit. 

(4) Any person becoming entitled to a unit consequent upon 
the death or bankruptcy of a unitholder may, upon pro¬ 
ducing such evidence as to his title, as the Trust shall consider 
sufficient, be paid the repurchase value of all units to the credit 
of the deceased at par or at such rate as may he determined 
by the Trust after the formalities in connection with the 
claim have been complied with by the claimant.. 

XVIII. Application on behalf of Minor 

(1) An adult individual being a parent, step-parent or other 
lawful guardian of a minor may apply for the units and deal 
with them In accordance with an to the extent provided in 
sub-section (2A) of Section 21 of the Act and in this scheme. 

(21 Such an adult while applying for units shall furnish to 
the Trust in such manner as mav be specified, proof of age 
of the minor and the capacity to hold and deal with units on 
behalf of the minor. 

Provided that the Trust shall be entitled to act on the state¬ 
ments made by such adult in the application form without 
any further proof. 

XIX. Transfer of Units :— 

(1) Every unitholder shall he entitled to transfer the units 
or any of the units held by him by an instrument in writ¬ 
ing in a form approved by the Chairman of the Trust provided 
that no transfer shall be registered if the registration thereof 
would result in the transferor or the transferee being a holder 
of a number of units not being a multiple of ten. 


(21 Every instrument of transfer shall be signed bv th« 
transferor and the transferee and the transferor shall be deem¬ 
ed to remain the holder of the units transferred until the name 
of the transferree is entered in the register in respect thereof. 

(3) Every instrument of transfer shall be duly stamped (if 
under the law it requires to be stamped and left with the Trust 
for registration along with the relevant unit certificate or* 
certificates and such other evidence as the Trust may require' 
in support of the title of the tiansferor or his right to transfer 
of units. For purposes of calculation of the value of stamps 
to bo affixed, the value of each unit shall be Rs. 10/'- i,e. at 
par. Tf the instrument of transfer is not adequately stamped, 
the Trust reserves the right to reject the instrument of 
transfer. 

(41 Every instrument of transfer shall be lodged with the 
Trust for registration at least a month before the period of 
closure of books (twice a year! along with the relevant certi¬ 
ficate. If the transfer is registered in the books of the Trust 
after the period of book closure as the case may be the divi¬ 
dend accruing for the relative half year will be paid to the 
transferor. 

(51 When the units are Issued in the official name, they 
shall he deemed to be transferred without any Instrument of 
transfer from each holder of the office to the succeeding holder 
of the office on and from the date on which the latter takes 
charge of the office. When the holder of the office transfers 
the units so held to a person not being this successor in office 
the transfers shall be made by an instrument of transfer 
signed by the holder of the office and the name of the office. 

XX. Nomination by unitholders — 

(11 Unitholders holding units singly or two unitholders 
holding jointly may exercise the right to make or cancel a 
nomination to the extent provided in the Regulations. 

(21 Unitholders being either parent or lawful guardian on 
behalf of a minor, an eligible institution, an applicant who 
has applied for units for the benefit of a mentally handicapped 
person shall have no right to make any nomination. 

XXI. Investment Limits :— 

(11 Investments by the Trust from the funds of tho Scheme 
in the securities of any one corporation shall not exceed 15% 
of the securities issued an outstanding of such corporations. 

Provided that the aggregate of such investments in the 
capital initially issued by new industrial undertakings shall not 
at any time exceed 5% of the total amount of the said funds. 

(21 The limits prescribed under sub-clause 01 shall not 
apply to investments of the Trust in bonds, deposite and 
debentures of a company whether secured or not. 

XXIT. Income Distribution :■—■ 

The Trust shall not declare any dividend under the scheme 
for the first three years of tho scheme. Thereafter dividend 
will be declared at the rate of 24% for the fourth and fifth 
year and 30% for the sixth and seventh years of the scheme 
respectively. 

The income distribution for each quarter shall be made pay¬ 
able at the begining of the quarter and will be paid by the 
Trust under such prepayment arrangments by means of 
income distribution warrants or any instrument encashablc at 
par at the branches of such bank as the Trust may specify, 
income Distribution warrants shall be forwarded to the Unit- 
holder in advance for the fourth, fifth sixth and seventh years 
by 1991. 

Every warrant shall have a validity period for three months. 
The Trust shall not be bound to pay interest in the event of 
any of the warrants not reaching the unitholders before the 
cxniry of validity period or in the event of their becoming 

stale. 

In the event of death of the unitholder if the sole nominee 
is eligible to bold the units and desires to continue to hold 
the units (hen the sole nominee shall be bound to return all

the unencashed warrants for the future quarters for necessary 
rectification. 

However, such a nominee desiring to continue to hold the 
units shall not be entitled to any interest or any compensation 
during the period it takes the Trust to rectify the warrants 
directly issued in favour of the deceased unitholder to those 
in favour of the newly admitted unitholder. 

XXIII. Publication of accounts ;— 

The Trust shall as soon as may he after the 30th lune of 
each year cause to be published in such manner as the Board 
may decide, accounts in the manner specified by the Board, 
showing the working of the scheme during the period ending 
on 30th June. The Trust shall, on request in writing received 
from a unitholder, furnish bint a copy of the accounts so pub¬ 
lished. 

XXTV. Additions ami amendments to scheme :— 

The Board may from time to time add to or otherwise 
amend this scheme and any amendment thereof will be noti¬ 
fied in the Official Gazette. 

XXV, Termination of the Scheme :— 

The Scheme shall stand finall y terminated depending upon 
the date of joinnig as given under :— 

16—31st August, 1990—16th August 1997 

01—30th September, 1990—01st September, 1997 

Of—31st October, 1990—01st October, 1997 

The outstanding units of the unitholders shall be repurchased 
and the unitholders shall be paid the value of their units at tho 
tepurchasc price fixed for the final repurchase during the above 
period. Besides receiving the repurchase price determined, 
no further benefit of any kind either by way of increase in 
the repurchase value or by way of dividend for any subse¬ 
quent period shall accrue and the repurchase value will be paid 
by the Trust as early as possible after the unit certificate with 


the form on the reverse thereof, duly completed has been re¬ 
ceived by it. 7’he Unit Certificate received for repurchase 
shall bo retained by the Trust for cancellation. 

XXVI. Scheme to be binding on unitholders :— 

The terms of this scheme, including any amendments there¬ 
of from time to time, shall be binding on each unitholder 
and every other person claiming through him as if he had 
expressly agreed that they shold be so binding. 

XXVII. Suspension or closure of sales :— 

Sale of units under this schemo may be suspended or closed 
by the Trust at any time after giving notice of seven day* in 
important daily newspapers of its intention to do so. 

XXVIII. Copy of Scheme to be made available :— 

A copy of this scheme incorporating all amendments thereto 
shall be made available for inspection at the offices of the 
Trust at all times during its business hours on payment of a 
sum of Rs. 5/-. 

XXIX. Benefits to the unitholders :— 

All benefits accruing under the Scheme in respect of capital 
reserves and surpluses if any available at the time of tho 
closure of the scheme shall be distributable only among the 
unitholders who hold the units at its closure 

XXX. Power to construe provisions :— 

Should any doubt arise as to the interpretation of any the 
provisions of the scheme, Chairman or in hig absence tho 
Executive Truslee shall have powers to construe the provisions 
of the Scheme, in so far such construction is not Ip any manner 
prejudicial or contrary to the basic structure of the Scheme 
and such decision shall be final and conclusive. 

XXXI. Relaxation /Variation/Modification of provisions :— 

The Chairman or in hi9 absence the Executive Trustee of the 
Trust in order to mitigate hardships or for smooth and easy 
operation of the Scheme, relax, vary or modify any of the pro¬ 
visions of the scheme in case of any unitholder, or class of 
unitholders upon such conditions as may be deemed expedient.

5 -YEAR DEFERRED INCOME UNIT SCHEME 

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 21 of the 
Unit Trust of India Act, 1963 (52 of 1963), the Board of 
tbd Unit Trust of India hereby makes the following Unit 
Scheme : 

I, Short Title and Commencement 

1. The Scheme shall be called “5-Year Deferred Income 
Unit Scheme." 

2. It shall come into force from August 16, 1990 shall be 
for a period of 5 years, 

3. The offer of units under the scheme will be open for 

21 months from August 16, 1990 to October 31, 1990 

(both days inclusive). Provided that the Chairman or the 
Executive Trustee may suspend the offer of units under 
the scheme at any time before tho expiry of the aforesaid 
period or extend the period of offer beyond the aforesaid 
period by giving a week’s notice in such newspapes as may 
be decided. 

II. Definitions :— 

In this scheme, unless the context otherwise requires— 

(a) The “Act” means the Unit Trust of India Act 
1963; 

(b) “acceptance date' with reference to an appli¬ 
cation made by an applicant to the Trust for sale 
of units by the Trust means the day on which the 


Trust, after being satisfied that such application 
is in order accepts the same; 

(c) “Applicant 71 under the scheme shall mean and in¬ 
clude individuals and eligible Institutions as more 
particularly described in paragraph IV hereof; 

(d; “eligible Institutions” means an eligible Trust de¬ 
fined under the Unit Trust of India General Regu¬ 
lations 1964 and shall include private trust created 
by an instrument in writing and which is irre¬ 
vocable. 

(e) “number of units” to be issued means the aggre¬ 
gate of the number of units sold and outstanding; 

(f) “recognised stock exchange” means a stock ex¬ 
change which is, for the time being recognised 
under tile Securities Contracts (Regulation) Act, 
1956 (42 of 1956) ; 

(g) “Regulations” means Unit Trust of India General 
Regulations 1964 made under Section 43 (1) of the 
Act; 

(h) “Society" means a Society Registered under the 
Societies Registration Act of 1860 or any other 
society established under any state or central law 
for the time being in force. 

(i) “Unit" means one undivided share of the face value 
of Rupees ten in the unit capital; 

(j) all other expressions not defined herein bu( defined in 
the Act shall have the respective meanings assigned 
to 'hem by the Act. 






I s art m—S ec. 4] THE GAZETTE OF INDIA, OCTOBER 27, 1^0 (KARTIKA 5, 194-2) 3001 


III. Face Value of each Unit :— 

The face value of each unit shall be ten rupees. 

IV. Application foi units 

(1| Application for units may be made by residents only 
viz. 

(a) individuals either singly or with another individual 
on joint/either or survivor basis. 

(b) a parent, step-parent or other lawful gaurdian on 
behalf of a minor. An application cannot be made 
by an adult and minor jointly. 

(c) an eligible institution as defined under the scheme. 

(d) a society as defined under the scheme. 

2, Applications shall be made in such form as may be ap¬ 
proved by the Chairman/Executive Trustee of the Trust. 

3, The payment for the Units applied for by an eligible per¬ 
son shall be made by him along with the application in cash, 
cheque or draft. Cheques and drafts Should be drawn on 
branches of banks within the city where the office at which 
the application is tendered is situated, If the payment is 
made by cheque, the acceptance date will, subject to such 
cheque being realised, be the date on which the cheque is 
received by the Trust or by a branch -of a designated bank. 
Tf payment is made by draft the acceptance date will, subject 
to such draft being realised, be the date of issue of such 
draft provided the application is received by the Trust or a 
branch of a designated bank within a reasonable time , 

4, A unit certificate will be sent by registered post with or 
Without acknowledgement due to the address given by the 
applicant and the Trust will not incur any liability for the loss, 
damage, misdelivery, or non-delivery of the certificate, so 
sent. 


oWatimtiing and deductfftg therefrom such sum "as in the 
opinion of the Trust is adequate to cover brokerage, commis¬ 
sion taxes, if any, Stamp duties and other charges in relation 
to realisation of investments by the Trust and other adjust¬ 
ments and The distribution to the unitholders of the assets in 
respect of the scheme. In such an event the repurchase 
price shall bear in addition to the par value the other distribu¬ 
table component of the asset per unit arrived by the Trust in a 
manner satisfactory to its auditors and as the Board may 
approve. 

IX. Publication of final repurchase price ;— 

(a) Upon termination of the Scheme in the manner pro¬ 
vided in clause XXV, hereof the Trust shall as early as possi¬ 
ble after determining the iinal requrchase price publish it in 
such manner as it may deem fit, 

X. Valuation oj assets pertaining to this Scheme :— 

(1) For the purpose of valuation of assets under sub- 
clause 02J of Clause IX, the assets shall be classified into fa) 
cash, (b) investments, and (c) other assets. 

(2) Investments shall be valued by taking : 

A. (a) the closnig prices on the stock exchange as on the 
working day on which the valuation is made of the securities 
held by the Trust pertaining to this Scheme, provided where 
a security is quoted on more than one stock exchange the 
manner of determining the price of such security shall be 
decided by the Trust. 

(bj where any investment was not, during tbe relevant 
period, dealt in, or quoted on any recognised stock exchange, 
such value, as the Trust may, in the circumstances consider 
to bo fair value of such investment and 


V. Offer of units 

The offer of units under the Scheme shall be open for a 
period of 21 months commencing from August 16, 1990 

to October 31, 1990 (both days inclusive). The Trust reserves 
the right to close the offer before the stipulated date. 
Applications rccevied after the close of business hours on 
3lst October, 1990 and subsequently at any of tbe offices 
of the Trust shall be deemed invalid and rejected. 

VI. Repurchase of units :— 

The trust shall not repurchase units during the currency 
of the scheme. 

VII. Restrictions on offer and repurchase of units : — 

Notwithstanding anything contained in any provision of 
the Scheme, the Trust shall not be under an obligation to 
offer or finally repurchase units 

(i) on such days as are not working days; and 

(j'i) during the period when the register of unitholders 
is closed in connection with (as notified by the 
Trust) the annual dosing of the books and accounts. 

Explanation : — 

For the purpose of this scheme, the term “working day’’ 
shall mean a day which has not been either (i) notified 
under the Negotiable Instruments Act, 1881, to be a public 
holiday in the State of Maharashtra or such other Btaies 
where the Trust has its offices; or (ii) notified by the Trust 
in the Gazette of India as a day on which the (Slice of the 
Trust will be closed. 

VIII. Offer and repurchase prices 

(1) The units shall be offered at Rs. 10/- during August 
16, 1990 to 3lsl October, 1990. 

(2) In the event of a termination Of the scheme in the 
manner as specified in Clause XXV hereof the Trust shall 
determine the repurchase price by valuing the assets per¬ 
taining to the scheme as at tbe dose of business on the date 
notified for termination reduced by the liabilities pertaining 
to the scheme and dividing them by the number ef units 


'B. (ar) -In the case of interest earning deposits, interest 
accrued "but not -received. 

(b) In the case of Government securities and debentures, 
interest accrued but not received, and 

i c) In the case of preference shares and equity shares 
quoted ex-dividend any dividend declared but not received. 

(3) Other assets shall be valued at their book value. 

XII. Manner of preparation of unit certificate :— 

Unit Certificates shall be in Form A annexed hereto. 
Each unit certificate shall bear a distinctive number, the 
number of units represented by the certificate and the name 
of the unitholder. 

Xil.Memrter oj preparation of unit certificate :— 

The Unit Certificates may be engraved or lithographed or 
printed as the Board may, from time to time, determine and 
shall be signed on behalf of the Trust by two persons duly 
authorised by the Trust. Every signature may either be 
autographic or may be effected by a mechanical method. No 
unit certificates shall be valid unless and until it is so Signed. 
Unit Certificates so signed shall be valid and binding not¬ 
withstanding that, before the issue thereof, any person whose 
signature appears thereon, may have ceased to be a person 
authorised to sign unit certificates on behalf of the Trust. 
Provided that should the unit certificate so prepared contain 
the signature of an authorised person who however is dead 
at .the lime of issue of the certificate; the Trust may by a 
method considered by it as most suitable, cancel the signa¬ 
ture of such a person appearipg on the certificate and have 
the signature of any other authorised person affixed to it. 
The Unit Certificate so issued shall also be valid. 

XIII. Trust not to be recognised regarding unit certificates :— 

The person who is registered as tbe holder and in whose 
nuffie a unit certificate has been issued shall be the only 

? arson to be recognised by the Trust a* the unitholder and as 
aving a right, title Or interest in or to such unit certificate 
and the units Which it represents; and the Trust may recog-

nise such unitholder as tho absolute owner thereof and shall 
not be bound by any notice to the contrary or to take notice 
of tho execution of any trust or save as herein expressly provi¬ 
ded or as by some court of competent jurisdiction affecting 
the title to any unit certificate or the units thereby represen¬ 
ted. 

XIV. Exchange of Unit Certificate and procedure when the 

certificate is mutilated , defaced, lost etc :— 

(,i) In case any unit certificate shall be mutilated or worn 
or defaced, the Trust at its discretion, may issue to the per¬ 
son entitled a new unit certificate representing the same 
aggregate number of units as tho mutilated or worn or defac¬ 
ed unit cej lilioate. In case any unit certificate should be 
lost, stolen or destroyed, the Trust may, at its discretion, 
issue to the person entitled a new certificate in lieu thereof no 
such now certificate shall be issued unless the applicant shall 
previously have. 

(i) Furnished to the Trust evidence satisfactory to it of 
the mutilation, wearing out, defacement loss, theft 
or destruction of the original unit certificate. 

tii) Paid all expenses in connection with the investga- 
tion of the facts. 

(iii) In case of mutilation or wearing out or defacement 
produced and surrendered to the Trust the mutilat¬ 
ed or worn out or defaced unit certificate; and 

(iv) furnished to the Trust such indemnity as it may 
require. The Trust shall not incur any liability for 
issuing such certificate in good faith under the pro¬ 
visions of this clause. 

(2) Before issuing any certificate under the provisions of 
this clause, the Trust may require the applicant for the unit 
certificate to pay a fee of Rupee one per unit certificate issued' 
by it together with a sum sulficienl in the opinion of the 
Trust to cover stamp duty, if any, or other charges or taxes 
including postal registration charges that may be payable in 
connection with the issue and dispatch of such certificate. 

XV. Register of unitholders 

The following provisions shall huvo effect with regard to 
the registration of unitholders : 

f 1) A r egister of unitholders shall be kept by the Trust 
at its Head Office and there shall be entered in tho 
register : 

(a) the name and addresses of the unitholders; 

(.b) the distinctive number of the unit certificate 
and the number of units held by every such 
person; and 

(c) the date on which such person became the 
holder of the units in his name. 

(.2) (a) If a unit certificate stands registered in the 
name of two persons, such persons shall be 
deemed to hold the certificates jointly and a 
dischargo by the person first named in the re¬ 
gister of the unitholders shall, a.s regards re¬ 
ceipt of amounts due in respect of such units, 
discharge the Trust in respect of such amounts. 

(b) Where two individuals, none of them being a 
minor, apply for issue of a unit certificate in 
their favour and request in the application that 
either of them should be permitted to deal with 
the units, the Trust shall record in its books 
suitatble entries to take note of such requests 
and when a unit certificate has been issued in 
such circumstances, then either of tho holders 
shall be entitled to deal with the units repre¬ 
sented by such certificate, and a discharge by 
either ol such persons shall, as regards receipt 
of amounts due in respect of such units, 
discharge the Trust in respect of such amounts.

Provided that the income distribution declared in respect of 
the units represented bv such certificates shall be paid to the 
person first named in the register of unitholders. 

(3) Anv change of name or address on the part of any 
unitholder shall be notified to the Trust, which, on being satis¬ 
fied of such change and on compliance with such formalities 
as it may require, shall alter the register accordingly. 

(4) Except when the register is closed in accordance with 
the provisions in that behnlf hereinafter contained, the register 
shall during business hours (subject to such reasonable res¬ 
trictions as the Trust may impose but so that not less than 
two hours on each business day shall be allowed for inspec¬ 
tion) be open to inspection by any unitholder without charge. 

(5) The register will be closed at such times and for period 
as the Trust may from time to time determine provided that 
it shall not be closed for more than 60 days in any one year; 
the Trust shall give notice of such closure bv advertisement 
in such newspaper as the Board may direct. 

XVI. Receipt by unitholder to discharge Trust 

The receipt of the unitholder for any monevs paid to him 
in respect of the units represented by the certificate shall he a 
good discharge to the Trust. 

XVII. Death or bankruptcy of a unitholder ;— 

(1) In case of death of either of the jonit holders of a unit 
certificate, the survivor shall be the only person recognised by 
the Trust as having title to or interest in the units represented 
bv the unit certificate. Provided that nothing herein con¬ 
tained shall affect any right which anv other person may have 
as against such survivor in respect of teh said units. 

(2) In the event of death of a single holder, the nominee 
shall be the person recognised by the Trust as the person en¬ 
titled to the amount payable bv the Trust in respect of units 
under the Regulations. 

(3) In the absenre of a valid nomination bv a single unit¬ 
holder, the executor or administrators of the deceased unit¬ 
holder or a holder of succession certificate issued under Part X 
of the Indian Succession Act. 1925 (39 of 1925) shall be the 
onlv persons who may be recognised by the Trust as having 
anv title to the unit. 

(4) Any person becoming entitled to a unit consequent 
upon the death or bankruptcy of it unitholder may, upon 
producing such evidence as to his title, as the Trust shall 
consider sufficient, be paid the repurchase value of all 
units to the credit of the deceased at par or at such rate 
as may be determined by the Trust after the formalities In 
connection with claim have been complied with by the 
claimant. 


XVIII. Application on behalf of Minor 

(1) An adult individual being a parent, step-parent or 
other lawful guardian of a minor may apply for tho units 
and deal with them in accordance with and to the extent 
provided in sub-section (2A) of section 21 of the Act and 
in this scheme. 

(2) Such an adult while applying for units shall furnish 
to the Trust in such manner as may be specified, proof of 
age of the minor and the capacity to hold and deal with 
units on behalf of the minor. 

Provided that the Trust shall be entitled to act on the 
statements made by such adult in the application form 
without any further proof. 

XIX. Transfer of Units :— 

(1) Every unitholder shall be entitled to transfer the 
units or any of the units held by him by an instrument in 
writing in a form approved by the Chairman of the Trust 
provided that no transfer shall be reeisered if the registrar 
tion thereof would result in the transferor or the transferee 
being a holder of a number of units not being a multiple 
of ten.

(2) Every instrument of transfer shall be signed by the 
transferor and the transferee and the transferor shall be 
deemed to remain the holder of the units transferred until 
the name of the transferee is entered in the register in 
respect thereof. 

(1) Every instrument of transfer shall be duly stamped 
(if under the law it requires to be stamped and left with 
the Trust for icgistralion along with the relevant unit 
certificate or certificates and such other evidence as the 
Trust may require in support of the title of the transferor 
or his right to transfer of units. For purposes of calculation 
of the value of stamps to be affixed, the value of each 
unit shall be Rs. 10/- i,e. at par. If the instrument of transfer 
is not adequately stamped, the Trust reserves the light to 
reject the instrument of transfer. 

(4) Every instrument of transfer shall be lodged with 
the Trust for registration at least a month before the period 
of closure of books (twice a year! along with the relevant 
certificate. If the transfer is registered in the books of tho 
Trust after the period of book closure as the case may be 
the dividend accruing for the relative half year will be 
paid to the transeferor. 

(51 When the units arc issued in the official name, they 
shall be deemed to be transferred without any instrument 
of transfer from each holder of the office' to the succeeding 
holder of the office on and from the date on which the 
latter takes charge of the office. When the holder of the 
office transfers the units so hold to a person not being 
this successor in office the transfers shall be made by an 
instrument of transfer signed by the holder of the office 
and the name of the office:. 

XX. Nomination by unitholders :— 

(1) Unitholders holding units singly or two unitholders 
holding jointly may exercise the right to make or cancel 
a nomination to the extent provided in the Regulations. 

(2) Uniholders being either parent or lawful guardian 
on behalf of a minor, an eligible institution, an applicant 
who has applied for units for the benefit of a mentally 
handicapped person shall have no right to make any 
nomination. 

XXI. Investment Limits :— 

(1) Investments by the Trust from the funds of the 
Scheme in the securities of any one corporation shall not 
exceed 15% of the securities issued and outstanding of 
such corporations. 

Provided that the aggregate of such investments in the 
capital initially issued by new industrial undertakings shall 
not at any time exceed 5% of the total amount of the 
said funds. 

(2) The limits prescribed under sub-clause (1) shall not 
apply to investments of the Trust in bonds, deposits and 
debentures of a company whether secured or not. 

XXII. Income Distribution :— 

The Trust shall not declare any dividend under the 
scheme for the first two years of the scheme. Thereafter 
dividend will be declarde at the rate of 18%, 24% and 
30% p. a. on quarterly basis for the third, fourth and 
fifth years of the scheme respectively. 

The income distribution for each quarter shall be made 
payable at the begining of the quarter and will be paid by the 
Trust under such prepayment arrangements by means of 
income distribution warrants or any instrument encashable 
at par at Ihe branches of such bank as the Trust may 
specify. Income Distribution warrants shall be forwarded 
to the Unitholder in advance for the third, fourth and 
fifth years by 1991. 

Every warrant shall have a validity period for three 
months. The Trust shall not be bound to pay interest in 
the event of anv of the warrants not reaching the uni¬ 
tholders hefore the exoiry of validity period or in the event 
of their becoming stale, 

In the event of death of the unitholder if the sole 
nominee is eligible to hold the units and desires to continue 


to hold the units then the sole nominee shall be bound to 
return all the unencushed warrants for the future quarters 
for necessary' i edification. 

llowcvci, such d nominee desiring to continue to hold 
the units shi ll not be entitled iu any interest or any com¬ 
pensation duimg the period it lattes the Trust to rectify the 
warrants directly issued in favour of the deceased unitholder 
to those iu favoui of the newly admitted unitholder. 

XXIJi. Publication of accounts :— 

The Trust shall as soon as may be after the 30th Juno 
of each year cause to be published in such manner as thq 
Board may decide, accounts in the manner specified by 
the Board, showing the working of the scheme during the 
period ending on 30th June. The Trust shall, on request in 
writing received from a unitholder, furnish him a copy of 
the accounts so published. 

XXTV. Additions and amendments to scheme :— 

The Board may from time to time add to or otherwise 
amend tills scheme and any amendment thereof will be 
notified in the Official Gazette. 

XXV. Termination of the Scheme :—• 

The Scheme shall stand finally terminated depending 
upon the date of joining as given under :— 

15—31st August, 1990—15th August, 1995 

01—30th September, 1990—1st September, 1995 

01—31st October, 1990—1st October, 1995 

The outstanding units of the unitholders shall be repur¬ 
chased and the unitholders shall he paid the value of their 
units at the repurchase price fixed for the final repurchase 
during the above period. Besides receiving the repurchase 
price determined, no further benefit of any kind either by 
way of increase in the repurchase value of by way of 
dividend for any subsequent period shall accrue and the 
repurchase value will be paid by tho Trust as early as 
possible after the unit certificate with the form on the 
reverse (hereof duly completed has been received by it. 
The Unit Certificate received for repurchase shall be 
retained by the Trust for cancellation. 

XXVI. Scheme to be binding on unitholders : — 

The terms of this scheme, including any amendments 
thereof from time to time, shall be binding on each 
unitholder and every other person claiming through him as 
if he. had expressly agreed that they should be so binding. 

XXVII. Suspension or closure of sales :— 

Salo of units under this scheme may be suspended or 
dosed by the Trust at any time after giving notice of 
seven days in important daily newspapers of its intention 
to do so. 

XXVIIJ. Copy of Scheme to be made available :— 

A copy of this scheme incorporating all amendments 
thereto shall be made available fop inspection at the offices 
of the Trust at ail times during it; business hours on pay¬ 
ment of a sum of Rs. 5/. 

XXIX. Benefits to the unitholders :— 

All benefits accruing under the Scheme in respect of 
capital reserves and surpluses if any available at the time 
of the closure of the scheme shall be distributable only 
among the unitholders who hold the units at its closure. 

XXX, Power to < oust me provisions: — 

Should any doubt arise as to the interpretation of any 
of the provisions of the scheme, Chairman or in his 
absence the Executive Trustee shall have powers to cons¬ 
true .the provisions of the Scheme, in so far such con¬ 
struction is not in any manner prejudicial or contrary to 
the basic structure of the Scheme and such decision shall 
be final and conclusive.

CANTONMENT BOARD 
Deolali Cantonment, the 21st September 1990 

S. R. O. 646/ADM/E-l.—Whereas a draft of certain 
bye-laws for the regulation of matters and things relating 
to the supply and use of water and the manner in which 
connections may be given by the Board from water supply 
installations in the Cantonment Board, Deolali was publi¬ 
shed with the Cantonment Board’s Notice No. 211-D/Water 
Bye-Laws/E-1 /918 dated 27th February, 1990 as required 
by section 284 of the Cantonments Act, 1924 (2 of 1924), 
for inviting objections and suggestions from the public till 
the expiry of a period of thirty days from the date of publi¬ 
cation of the said notice. 

And Whereas the said notice was put on the Notice 
Board of Deolali Cantonment on 27th Feb, 1990. 

And Whereas no objections or suggestions were received 
from the public by the Cantonment Board before the said 
period; 

AND Whereas, the Central Government have duly approved 
and confirmed the said draft of Bye-laws as required by sub¬ 
section (1) of section 284 of the said Act; 

Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by 
clauses (32), (33) and (34) of section 282 and section 283 
12—29901/90 


of the said Act, the Cantonment Board, Deolali hereby melt#) 
the following bye-laws namely :— 

1. WATER SUPPLY BYE LAWS 

Short Title and Commencement 

(1) These bye-laws may be called 'The Deolali Canton* 
ment Water Supply Bye Laws 1990’. 

(2) They shall come into force on tbo date of their 
publication in the Official Gazette. 


2. Definitions 

In these bye laws unless the context otherwise require ;— 

(a) ‘Board’ means the Cantonment Board, Deolalj. 

(b) ‘Commercial connection’ means a connection given 
for the purpose other than domestic and non-domes¬ 
tic purposes such as restaurants, hotels, factories, 
Cinema houses, petrol pumps, Railway administra¬ 
tion and other Central/State Government depart¬ 
ments and other connection of like nature. 

(c) ’Consumer’ means a person or a Manager of a 
flmi/Institution, who takes water connection from 
the Board .for demostic, non demostic or commercial 
use. 



3006 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA, OCTOBER 27, 1990 (KARTIKA 5, 1912) [Part III— Sec. 4 


(d) ‘Distribution Pipe’ includes any water main under 
the control of the Board for the purpose of supply¬ 
ing water within its jurisdiction. 

(e) ‘Domestic connection’ means a connection given 
purely for domestic purpose and religious purpose. 

(f) ‘Executive Officer’ means Executive Officer of the 
Board. 

(g) ‘Form’ means a form appended to these Bye-laws. 

(h) ‘Lessee’ means a person, who has taken or lease 
land or building on rent either from the Government 
or private person and includes a tenant, 

(i) ‘Non Domestic connection’ means a connection 
other than domestic or commercial connection, 
which is given for the purpose such as schools, dis¬ 
pensaries, parks and gardens and other connections 
of like nature, 

(j) ’Occupier’ Includes an owner In occupation or other¬ 
wise using his own land or building, 

(k) ‘Owner* includes any person, who is receiving or 
is entitled to receive the rent of any building or 
land whether on his own account or on behalf of 
himself and others or an agent or trustee or who 
would so- receive the rent or be entitled to receive 
it, if the building or land were let to a tenant. 

(l) ‘Service Pipe’ means the connection, which is 
with the distribution pipe under the control of the 
Board for the purpose of supplying of water to 
the premises for demestic purposes. 

Cm) Words and expressions’ used herein and not defin¬ 
ed but defined in the Cantonments Act, 1924 (2 
of 1924) shall have the meanings respectively 
assigned to them, in that Act. . 

3. Restrictions for use of water 

No person shall bath or wash any animal, clothing, wool, 
leather, skins utensils or any other things at any public stand 
post or hydrant or use water for building purposes or other¬ 
wise wilfully cause a waste thereof. 

4. Authority to operate public Institutions 

No person other than a person duly authorised by the 
Board or the Executive Officer for the purpose shall open or 
inspect or in any way interfere with any pipe, valve, plug, 
or other fittings belonging to or appertaining to any public 
installation other than a tap for drawing water. 

5. Tampering with water tap or stand post 

No person shall tamper with any water tap, stand post, 
or hydrant belonging to the Board so as to cause any 
damage to the mechanism or working of such tap, stand 
post or hydrant. 

6. Restriction on wilful waste of water 

No person shall wilfully waste or cause or allow to be 
wasted any water from any pipe, public stand post, hydrant, 
value or fitting or shalll cause It to be tapped by means of 
rubber pipe or any other contrivance to be diverted through 
any other channel. 

7. Manner of drawing water 

All water obtained from the public stand post or hydrant 
shall be carried there from in clean vessels. 

8. Application for connection 

Every application for the supply of water under these bye 
laws shall be in writing signed bv the owner of the building 
or land in form ‘A’ and the work necessary for such supply 
shall not be commenced until the applicant had deposited 
with the Executive Officer, such estimated cost Of expendi¬ 
ture inclusive of road cutting and reonstatemeOt charges 
and before such date as may be fixed by him In this behalf. 


9. Connection charges 

An application under bye law N. 8 shall be accompanied 
by an amount of Rs. 50/- as connection charges. 

10. Supervision charges 

The estimated cost of expenditure referred to in bye law 
No. 8 shall also include a percentage of 20 percent of the 
actual cost of work to cover supervision charges, 

11. Metered supply 

A water connection for every premise in which water 
would be consumed for domestic or commercial purposes shall 
be permitted on the metered system and the pipe connection 
granted prior to the date of commencement of these bye 
laws shall be converted into the metered system gradually 
as may be decided by the Board from time to time. 

12. Laying pipe line etc. 

In every case in which a now service connection with the 
distribution pipe of the Board Is made or an existing connec¬ 
tion required renewal or repair or alteration or extension, all 
necessary communication pipes and fittings of standard spe¬ 
cifications CIST) from and incluidng stop cock upto the 
nearest distribution main shall be supplied by the consumer 
at his cost and the work of laying such communication pipes 
and fittings upto the stop cock shall be executed by the 
Board but the cost of such work shall be met by the consumer, 
who shall be liable to pay the said sum in advance before 
the connection is made or renewed. The service pipe con¬ 
nection so laid including stop cock shall remain the property 
of the Board. 

13. Size of Service Connection 

(i) The service pipe connection for domestic supply shall 
be of such a bore not less than 12 milimeters (i") and not 
more than 20 milimeters (I") in diameter any may 
extend to 40 milimeters in diameter in case of non domestic 
or commercial supplies as may be determined by the Exe¬ 
cutive Officer in each case. 

fii) The size of the ferrule shall bo less in size than the bore 
of the service pipe. 

(iii) No service pipe connection shall be opened into a 
latrine or be laid through it. No structure or obstruction 
shall be built over the hurried service pipe. 

14. Power to renew or alter service connection 

(1) No service pipe connection with the distribution pipe of 
the Board shall be made, renewed, altered or extended except 
by a written authority of the Executive Officer and until the 
certificate specified in bye law 16 has been given. 

(ii) The consumer shall not renew, alter or extend the 
service pipe connection inside or outside his house /premises 
without the previous permission in writing of the Executive 
Officer. 

15. Provision of pipes or fitting inside premises 

fi) Communication pipes and fittings required for pro¬ 
viding domestic supply connection beyond the stop cock 
inside the premises shall be provided by the consumer at 
his cost. 

Cii) The pipe line shall comply with IS 1239 (Part I) 1968 
and the fittings shall comply with IS 1239 (part II) 1969. 

16. Execution of the work by licensed plumber 

411 communication pipes an<1 fittings beyond stop cock 
referred to in bye law 15 shall be laid and applied by a 
plumber duly licensed bv the Board under the direct super¬ 
vision of the Engineer of the Board who shall give and sign 
a certificate free of charge when such communication pines 
and all necessary fittings and work have been laid supplied, 
executed in satisfactory manner and sufficient arrangements 
have been made for draining of waste water. 



Part III—Sec. 4] THE GAZETTE OF INDIA, OCTOBER 27, 1990 (KARTIKA 5, 1912) 3007 


17. Supply of meter 

(,i) Meters shall be supplied by the Board. An amount 
of Its. 500/- should be deposited with the Board before 
installation of meter, 

(ii) Existing meters will remain property of the Board 
after duly examined by the Board. 

18. Maintenance of meters 

(i) The owner or lessee or occupier as the case may be 
shall be responsible tor repairs or theft etc. when damage 
or theft to nis meter is caused and if repairs or replace¬ 
ment necessary thereto are not arranged within 15 days of 
the issue of the notice in this behali, the connection shall 
temain cut off until the meter is repaired or replaced to 
the satisfaction of the Cantonment Executive Officer. 

Cii) The reconnection charges payable to the Board shall 
be its. 50/-. 

19. Meter readings 

(i) All water meters shall be looked and sealed by the em¬ 
ployees of the Board authorised in this behalf and meter read¬ 
ing shall be taken once in a month by a person appointed by 
the Executive Officer in the presence as far as possible of re¬ 
presentative of the consumer of water supply. 

(ii) Meter readings shall be recorded in a water meter read¬ 
ing book maintained by the Board in this behalf as well as in 
a meter reading card which is with the consumer. 

20. Meter not to be tampered 

No meter shall in any way be tampered with by the con¬ 
sumer or any Person on his behalf. The duty ol safeguard¬ 
ing the meter from the loss by theft or pilferage will devolve 
on the consumer, who will otherwise be called upon to make 
good the loss. 

21. Number of water taps 

Normally not more than two taps in case of metered supply 
shall be given to any premises unless special circumstances 
exist at the discretion of the Cantonment Executive Oftirer on 
the grounds of area, locality, pressure of water, situation and 
use for which water is required. 

22. Authority to close, reduce, stop water supply 

The Board shall be at liberty in its discretion to close, stop, 
reduce or restrict the supply of water either in respect of 
quantity or hours of supply or premises in the event of default 
of any of the provisions of these bye laws or in the event of 
receipt of i educed supply of water from the principal source 
or drought or other unavoidable causes as the case may be 
and the Board shall not be liable to pav any damages for any 
such act or commission. 

23. Security deposit atid payment of charges by consumers 

(i) All consumers for water supply shall deposit with the 
Board a scrurity deposit as under : — 

(a) for each domestic conneclion—Rs. 1000/- 

(b) for each non domestic connection—Rs. 2000/- 

(c) for each commercial connection.—Rs, 5000/-. 

(ii) The monthly charges for the water shall be payable 
within 10 (ten) days from tbe date of issue of the bill for the 
same. 

(iii) If the consumer fails to make payment of the said 
charges as required under clause (ii) above, the same shall be 
deducted from the security deposit or otherwise the supply of 
water shall be disconnected within seven days after issue ol 
notice by the Executive Officer in this regard. 

24. Correctness of meter reading 

(i) Tn case of any doubt about the correctness of the read¬ 
ing reroeded under bye law' 19 for any particular month the 
concerned ow'ncr, occupier of the building or land may within 
7 (seven) days of the issue of tbe bill for the payment of 
charges for the water consumer request the Executive Officer, 
in writing to test the water meter. 


(ii) If on the test referred to in clause (i) the water meter 
proved to be less than 5 percentum fast, the charges for test¬ 
ing water meter referred to in clause (iii) shall be paid by 
the applicant in any other case such charges shall not be paid 
by him and estimated over charges pro-rate for the month in 
respect of which the accuracy of meter is disputed shall be 
adjusted by the Executive Officer. 

(iii) The charges for testing the water meter shall be 
Rs, 10/- (ten). 

25. Fixation of charges when meter give incorrect reading 

In case where a water meter is found to give incorrect read¬ 
ing or is out of order for any period exceeding one month 
the average consumption of water recorded during the proceed¬ 
ing three months or where such record is not available such 
date as the Executive Officer considers most suitable shall be 
deemed to be the actual consumption of water till Ihe meter is 
put in order, tested and rectified. 

26. Penalty 

(1) Whoever— 

(a) uses for otherwise than domestic purposes any water 
supply by the Board for domestic use, or 

(b) where water is supplied bv agreement with the Board 
for a specified purpose uses that water for any other 
purpose shall be punishable with fine which may 
extend to two hundred and fifty rupees and the 
Board shall be entitled lo recover from him the 
price of water misused. 

(ii) If any person contravenes any provisions of these bye 
laws, he shall be punishable with the fine which may extent 
to one hundred rupees and where a contravention is a conti¬ 
nuing one with an additional fine which may extend to twenty 
rupees for every day during which surh contravention conti¬ 
nues after conviction of the first such contravention. 

27. Authority for permanent disconnection or heavy fine 

Cantonment Executive Officer is entitled for disconnecting 
any connection permanently or to charge heavy fine to an 
amount which he feels suitable to the said consumers, whose 
connection is found to be fitted with an illegal electric motor/ 
pump, 

28. The consumer shall Pay to the Cantonment Board for 
the quantity of water consumed at the rate as set out below ;■— 

All in costed rate of the Station at which the consumer is 
charged for the year shall be on the basis of ‘All-in-costed 
rate' of the installation for pumping supply and proportionate 
distribution for preceeding financial year. For instance the 
costed rate for 1987-88 will be effective from 1st April 1989 
instead of from 1st April, 1988. 

29. Depending on the availability of surplus water and in 
case of the natural calamities, the Cantonment Board can 
enter into the agreement with the requiring body for supply 
of the filtered water and the modalities of the agreement can 
be worked out by the Cantonment Executive Officer. How¬ 
ever. such water would be charged at the rate of non¬ 
domestic. 

30. As per the directions of the World Bank to minimise 
the wastage of water, public stand posts can be provided to 
15—20 houses on the names of the houses, which could be 
asked to share the metered charges for the consumption of 
water and would be responsible for the maintenance. How¬ 
ever, only relaxation could be the exemption of the deposits, 
which would effectively control the consumption of water and 
so also raise the awareness amongst the public. Such stand 
posts will be at the public place and would not be anybody’s 
personal property and should be kept to the bearest mini¬ 
mum. 

31. Bye-laws can be changed at the directions of the Board 
or on the directions of the higher authorities, and same shall 
apply to all consumers against which no appeal shall lie,
